← wa30 的世界模型

它是怎么学出来的

agent 在前几次上机时边玩边把看到的规律写成可检验的代码：每条都对接下来的每一步自动判对错。它一共写过 37 条，其中 6 条被后来的画面推翻并撤掉，留下 31 条。通关之后，这些说法在最后一次上机的录像上被重新量过，合并成首页的规则；有几条说的是地形或画法，并进了「世界由什么组成」，不在下面的清单里。

每条规则的来历

  • 手空时按方向键：转头，能走就走一格
    由这些早期说法合并而来：action1_up、action2_down、action3_left、action4_right、head_faces_last_move、mole_blocks_move、stolen_donut_blocks_move
  • 攥着货时按方向键：人和货一起挪
    由这些早期说法合并而来：action1_up、action2_down、action3_left、action4_right、head_faces_last_move、donut_drag_follows、mole_blocks_move、stolen_donut_blocks_move
  • 按动作键：松手、攥住或打掉贼
    由这些早期说法合并而来：donut_grab_toggle、action5_inert_without_target、action5_vanishes_adjacent_mole、killed_mole_drops_cargo
  • 玩家动完，鼹鼠轮流各动一下
    由这些早期说法合并而来：mole_steal_on_block、mole_carry_above、mole_drop_in_capsule、mole_drop_target_by_skin、donut_take_back_from_mole、thief_strips_drag、grab_refused_by_still_thief、drag_released_by_thief_block、thief_step_rule、thief_patrol_next
  • 每按一次键，计时条按比例变短
    由这些早期说法合并而来：timer_bar_drains、bar_left_means_alive
  • 步数用完而没过关，游戏结束
    由这些早期说法合并而来：timer_bar_drains、bar_left_means_alive
  • 过关，当且仅当所有甜甜圈都安放进了容器
    由这些早期说法合并而来：levelup_on_release_iff_all_seated、win_needs_all_donuts_home
  • 重开之后，回到这一关开局的那一帧不进 Lean 模型
    由这些早期说法合并而来：—

被推翻的说法

「这一关打不通」，直到一颗甜甜圈落进容器

blocking_no_levelup_from_exploredwin_fill_capsule
  1. 原来以为

    第一次上机的第 1 关，agent 把地图走了个遍，攥、拖、松手都试过，就是不换关。它写下一条阻断主张：用已知的动作，从哪里出发都走不到换关；还把那个蓝框容器当成了会致死的陷阱——那几次其实是计时条走完了。

  2. 画面打脸

    那次上机的第 556 步，画面已经换关：前一步刚把最后一颗甜甜圈拖进容器、松手。

  3. 后来改成

    阻断主张撤掉，换成「本关全部甜甜圈都进了容器就换关」。这条后来又被改写了两次（见附录），成为首页那条过关规则。

鼹鼠驮货，不一定驮在头顶

mole_carry_above
  1. 原来以为

    头一回看见鼹鼠偷货：它走到甜甜圈正下方停一步，之后那颗货一直悬在它正上方一格，跟着它走。于是写下：被偷的货永远在鼹鼠正上方。

  2. 画面打脸

    后来一只鼹鼠从左侧偷走一颗货，那颗货跟在它右边走。

  3. 后来改成

    偷到手那一刻，货和鼹鼠的相对位置就固定下来，一路保持。最终的鼹鼠规则把鼹鼠和它驮的货当成两格，一起沿最短路挪。

附录

附录保留仓库里的原始叫法，关号从 0 开始（L0 = 正文第 1 关）。

另外 2 个改写的故事

过关看的是松手，不是位置

win_fill_capsulewin_no_empty_socketlevelup_on_release_iff_all_seated
  1. 原来以为

    「全部甜甜圈都进了容器就换关」。

  2. 画面打脸

    两处对不上。容器格被货盖满以后，画面上认不出哪几格是容器，这条在一次换关上判错，于是改成数「还看得见的空位」；后来又有一关，所有甜甜圈都已坐在容器格上却没换关——最后那颗还攥在手里，下一步一松手才换关。

  3. 后来改成

    改成：一步走完时手上没攥着、没有鼹鼠驮着货、每一颗都坐在容器格上，才过关。

挡不挡人，按结构认，不按颜色认

capsule_walkablecolor2_blocks_move_unless_pure2
  1. 原来以为

    早期两关里玩家能走进容器，于是写下：容器是可以站的地面。

  2. 画面打脸

    另一关里有一片颜色相近的格子，玩家走不进去，甜甜圈却能被拖进去。先改成按关分开说，又改成「含某种颜色、但不是纯色的格挡人」，这条后来也撤掉了。

  3. 后来改成

    最终按格子的结构分出六种地形：带斑点的围栏挡人不挡货，容器的边框和内芯人和货都能进，墙挡一切。

全部 37 条早期说法的每一版与源码

"""ACTION1 要么让玩家块（me 定位的 4x4 块：色0 顶行 + 色14 余下 3 行）上移恰好 CELL 格、另一轴不变，要么整帧不动。"""

NOTE = ""

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if prev == nxt:
        return "TRUE"
    return "TRUE" if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) == (-CELL, 0) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    from _lib import BG
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):          # 抠走旧块，涂回底色
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = BG
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):          # 原样画到新位置
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = prev[a[0] + dr][a[1] + dc]
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):

        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if prev == nxt:
        return "TRUE"
    return "TRUE" if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) == (-CELL, 0) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None

        return None

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dc in range(CELL):
        out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0            # 头贴上边（上移后头朝上）
        for dr in range(1, CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14  # 身体补满其余
    return out

"""ACTION1 让玩家块（4x4 整块）上移恰好 CELL 格、列不变，或整块不动（含撞靶激活那步：靶变色但块不动）。"""

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, paint_donut
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION1"):   # 拖行甜甜圈移进玩家旧格（第1关步644实测）
        out.update(paint_donut(cell[0], cell[1], 0))
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION1")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION1"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION1")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION1"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成（v10）
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION1")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION1") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来，这格不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成（v10）
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION1")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION1") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来，这格不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088）

"""ACTION1 让玩家块（4x4 整块）上移恰好 CELL 格、列不变，或整块不动（含撞靶激活那步：靶变色但块不动）。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION1；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA"
NOTE = "v3：不动分支从「整帧相同」改成「块 bbox 不变」——撞靶步块不动但靶 4→3、头转向，整帧不相同；v4：apply 目的块出帧（a[0]-4<0，贴顶被挡）时返回 None 不生成；v8：拖行改走 drag_paint——擦甜甜圈旧位残影、画新位（合成帧串链只画不擦，旧位留影会让下一步看到两个环0）；v9：apply 拖行态改用 block_top_left 定位——me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被环0甜甜圈撑大，玩家与甜甜圈不同行/列时整块糊错（第3关步785实测12格）；v10：apply 旧格四邻有鼹鼠/被驮货时不画旧格——鼹鼠会走进刚空出来的格（第7关步1289、1290 实测，终局卡第4关步2064 押1实12）；v11：旧格底图没依据时也不押——见 _lib.bg_unsure，终局卡第7关步2417（第12次意外）教的"
EVIDENCE = ["b522df4c37607269", "173db4790f8be282", "ece10e49ed76be4b", "7d05e728bd442864"]

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成（v10）
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION1")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION1") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088）

from _lib import me, CELL, donuts, block_top_left


def _pos(g):
    """玩家块像素左上：拖行时（帧里有环0）me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被货撑歪，改用 block_top_left。"""
    if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(g)):
        return block_top_left(g)
    return me(g)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = _pos(prev), _pos(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (-CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION1"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成（v10）
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴顶被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "up"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION1")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION1") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] - CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "up"           # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088）

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"

    if a is None or b is None:

    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    from _lib import BG
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = BG
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = prev[a[0] + dr][a[1] + dc]
    return out

    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None

    if a is None:
        return None

"""ACTION2 让玩家块（4x4 整块）下移恰好 CELL 格、列不变，或整块不动。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION2；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA"
NOTE = "v1：早期误读成「头/身分离下落」，实为整块下移+头转向下（头朝向是另一条概念的事）；位移以块 bbox 为准；v3：apply 目的块出帧（a[0]+7>63，贴底被挡）时返回 None 不生成"

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import BG
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = BG

    return out

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, paint_donut
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2"):   # 拖行甜甜圈移进玩家旧格（第1关步644实测）
        out.update(paint_donut(cell[0], cell[1], 0))

            for dc in range(CELL):

    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14

            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0

        return None

        return None

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):

        return "NA"

        return "NA"

        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

"""ACTION2 让玩家块（4x4 整块）下移恰好 CELL 格、列不变，或整块不动。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION2；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA"
NOTE = "v1：早期误读成「头/身分离下落」，实为整块下移+头转向下（头朝向是另一条概念的事）；位移以块 bbox 为准；v3：apply 目的块出帧（a[0]+7>63，贴底被挡）时返回 None 不生成；v7：拖行改走 drag_paint——擦甜甜圈旧位残影、画新位（合成帧串链只画不擦，旧位留影会让下一步看到两个环0）；v8：apply 拖行态改用 block_top_left 定位——me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被环0甜甜圈撑大，玩家与甜甜圈不同行/列时整块糊错（第3关步785实测12格）；v9：apply 旧格四邻有鼹鼠/被驮货时不画旧格——终局卡第4关步2064 一只鼹鼠从隔壁走进刚空出来的格（押1实12，终局卡第一次意外）；语料里鼹鼠进旧格 3 次，prev 上都贴着旧格"
EVIDENCE = ["7100c3a5f5159465", "9d323c883cd44b5c"]

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来，这格不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来，这格不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None

        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位

        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088 押14/0 实7，8 格）

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088 押14/0 实7，8 格）

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088 押14/0 实7，8 格）

from _lib import me, CELL, donuts, block_top_left


def _pos(g):
    """玩家块像素左上：拖行时（帧里有环0）me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被货撑歪，改用 block_top_left。"""
    if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(g)):
        return block_top_left(g)
    return me(g)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = _pos(prev), _pos(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088 押14/0 实7，8 格）

from _lib import me, CELL, donuts, block_top_left


def _pos(g):
    """玩家块像素左上：拖行时（帧里有环0）me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被货撑歪，改用 block_top_left。"""
    if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(g)):
        return block_top_left(g)
    return me(g)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = _pos(prev), _pos(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (CELL, 0)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION2"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[0] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 高帧：贴底被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "down"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION2")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION2") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0] + CELL, a[1])
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "down"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088 押14/0 实7，8 格）

"""ACTION3 要么让玩家块（me 定位的 4x4 块）左移恰好 CELL 格、另一轴不变，要么整帧不动。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION3；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA"
NOTE = ""
EVIDENCE = ["bea220486f78fc4d"]

from _lib import me, CELL, BG


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if prev == nxt:
        return "TRUE"
    return "TRUE" if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) == (0, -CELL) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = BG
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = prev[a[0] + dr][a[1] + dc]
    return out

        return None

            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)

        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "left"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"

        return "NA"

        return "NA"

def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴左边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, paint_donut
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "left"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION3"):   # 拖行甜甜圈移进玩家旧格（第1关步644实测）
        out.update(paint_donut(cell[0], cell[1], 0))
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "left"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向

            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0

    else:

    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return "NA"

    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"

    if d in ((0, 0), (0, -CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴左边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "left"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION3")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION3"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "left"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴左边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "left"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)

    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION3"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "left"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":

            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0

    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)

    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION3")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION3") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来，这格不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "left"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

"""ACTION3 让玩家块（4x4 整块）左移恰好 CELL 格、行不变，或整块不动。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION3；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA"
NOTE = "v3：断言对象从色0 头改成整块 bbox——头随朝向换边（左移后头在左），色0 单独定位列差不是 CELL；块不动（含撞墙）也算 TRUE；v6：apply 目的块出帧（a[1]-4<0，贴左边被挡）时返回 None 不生成；v10：拖行改走 drag_paint——擦甜甜圈旧位残影、画新位（合成帧串链只画不擦，旧位留影会让下一步看到两个环0，步646实测）；v11：apply 拖行态改用 block_top_left 定位——me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被环0甜甜圈撑大，玩家与甜甜圈不同行/列时整块糊错（第3关步785实测12格：玩家(8,9)甜甜圈(9,8)，联合bbox top-left 落到(8,8)）；v-next：apply 旧格四邻有鼹鼠/被驮货时不画旧格——鼹鼠会走进刚空出来的格（终局卡第4关步2064 ACTION2 押1实12；语料里 3 次都是 prev 贴着旧格）"
EVIDENCE = ["bea220486f78fc4d", "9c53253544a7a8a8", "900bcacebb131111"]

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, -CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴左边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "left"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION3")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION3") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来，这格不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "left"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088）

"""ACTION3 让玩家块（4x4 整块）左移恰好 CELL 格、行不变，或整块不动。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION3；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA"
NOTE = "v3：断言对象从色0 头改成整块 bbox——头随朝向换边（左移后头在左），色0 单独定位列差不是 CELL；块不动（含撞墙）也算 TRUE；v6：apply 目的块出帧（a[1]-4<0，贴左边被挡）时返回 None 不生成；v10：拖行改走 drag_paint——擦甜甜圈旧位残影、画新位（合成帧串链只画不擦，旧位留影会让下一步看到两个环0，步646实测）；v11：apply 拖行态改用 block_top_left 定位——me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被环0甜甜圈撑大，玩家与甜甜圈不同行/列时整块糊错（第3关步785实测12格：玩家(8,9)甜甜圈(9,8)，联合bbox top-left 落到(8,8)）；v-next：apply 旧格四邻有鼹鼠/被驮货时不画旧格——鼹鼠会走进刚空出来的格（终局卡第4关步2064 ACTION2 押1实12；语料里 3 次都是 prev 贴着旧格）；v-next2：旧格底图没依据时也不押——见 _lib.bg_unsure，终局卡第7关步2417（第12次意外）教的；v-next3：拖行落点紧贴皮15 贼时只押芯不押环外圈——见 _lib.thief_may_strip 与概念 thief_strips_drag，贼原地不动那一步会把货打脱手（环0→3），终局卡第7关步2474 第13次意外；v-next4（终局卡第8关步3250，tid a3f2a39e754ef8da，第20次意外，反例打在 action4_right 上）：**assert_ 也改用 block_top_left 定位拖行态**。v11 早写明 me() 在拖行时被环0 撑歪，却只改了 apply、没改 assert_——生成器和检查器读同一个位置用了两个词。那一步人走成恰好一格、货同时被贴着落点的贼打脱手（环0→3），me(prev) 的 bbox 含货、me(nxt) 只剩人，差出来一格对角判 FALSE。四条移动概念的 assert_ 是同一段代码，一起改；这条上那个 tid 判 NA，只是登记来源"
EVIDENCE = ["bea220486f78fc4d", "9c53253544a7a8a8", "900bcacebb131111", "a3f2a39e754ef8da"]

from _lib import me, CELL, donuts, block_top_left


def _pos(g):
    """玩家块像素左上：拖行时（帧里有环0）me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被货撑歪，改用 block_top_left。"""
    if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(g)):
        return block_top_left(g)
    return me(g)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = _pos(prev), _pos(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, -CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION3"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] - CELL < 0:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴左边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "left"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION3")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION3") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] - CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "left"         # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088）
当前版本源码

ACTION4 要么让玩家块（me 定位的 4x4 块）右移恰好 CELL 格、另一轴不变，要么整帧不动。
v1 源码
"""ACTION4 要么让玩家块（me 定位的 4x4 块）右移恰好 CELL 格、另一轴不变，要么整帧不动。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION4；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA"
NOTE = ""
EVIDENCE = ["69b5767e306ad0a5"]

from _lib import me, CELL, BG


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if prev == nxt:
        return "TRUE"
    return "TRUE" if (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) == (0, CELL) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = BG
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = prev[a[0] + dr][a[1] + dc]
    return out

"""ACTION4 让玩家块右移 CELL 格（列增加），或整帧不动。"""
LEVEL = "L1"
SCOPE = "只看 ACTION4；换关（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA；到边界时整帧不动返回 TRUE"

from _lib import me, CELL, BG


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    if prev == nxt:
        return "TRUE"  # 边界阻挡
    # 只断言列向右移
    col_diff = b[1] - a[1]
    if col_diff == CELL:
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"

    return None

        return None

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:

    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import BG
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = BG
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)

        for dc in range(CELL - 1):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, paint_donut
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION4"):   # 拖行甜甜圈移进玩家旧格（第1关步644实测）
        out.update(paint_donut(cell[0], cell[1], 0))
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"

    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint
    face = head_in_block(prev) if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev)) else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION4")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION4"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION4")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION4"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION4")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION4"):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION4")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION4") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来，这格不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return out

from _lib import me, CELL


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = me(prev), me(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION4")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION4") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088）
v17 · 2026-09-17T18:16:36 · L1

from _lib import me, CELL, donuts, block_top_left


def _pos(g):
    """玩家块像素左上：拖行时（帧里有环0）me() 的色0∪色14 bbox 被货撑歪，改用 block_top_left。"""
    if any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(g)):
        return block_top_left(g)
    return me(g)


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return "NA"
    a, b = _pos(prev), _pos(nxt)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return "NA"
    d = (b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
    if d in ((0, 0), (0, CELL)):
        return "TRUE"
    return "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION4"):
        return None
    from _lib import bg_px, bg_unsure, donuts, head_in_block, held_new_cells, drag_paint, block_top_left, move_refused, mole_may_enter, mask_timer_row
    if move_refused(prev, action, ctx):
        return None       # 玩家目的格是 2 格：整步被拒，这条不生成
    dragging = any(ring == 0 for _, _, ring in donuts(prev))
    a = block_top_left(prev) if dragging else me(prev)   # 拖行时 me() 被环0撑歪
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a[1] + 2 * CELL - 1 > 63:   # 目的块越出 64 宽帧：贴边被挡，这步不生成
        return None
    face = head_in_block(prev) if dragging else "right"
    bg = bg_px(prev, a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    out = {}
    drag_paint(prev, out, a, "ACTION4")   # 拖行：擦甜甜圈旧位、画新位
    cell = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    if cell not in held_new_cells(prev, "ACTION4") and not mole_may_enter(prev, cell) and not bg_unsure(prev, *cell):   # 甜甜圈进旧格时那格已是甜甜圈；隔壁有鼹鼠时它可能走进来；底图没依据时（贴着巢/容器边又认不下来）这格也不押
        for dr in range(CELL):
            for dc in range(CELL):
                out[(a[0] + dr, a[1] + dc)] = bg[dr][dc]
    b = (a[0], a[1] + CELL)
    for dr in range(CELL):
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + dc)] = 14
    face = face or "right"        # 拖行头不转（第1关步642实测），否则头朝移动方向
    if face == "up":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0], b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "down":
        for dc in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + CELL - 1, b[1] + dc)] = 0
    elif face == "left":
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1])] = 0
    else:
        for dr in range(CELL):
            out[(b[0] + dr, b[1] + CELL - 1)] = 0
    return mask_timer_row(out)      # 最底一行格的第 4 像素行是计时条，不押（终局卡第4关步2088）

def _pos(g):

        return block_top_left(g)
    return me(g)

    return "FALSE"

动作键打空，除计时外什么都不变
ACTION5 打空时（玩家四邻没有鼹鼠、没有被搬的货、没有环3 的甜甜圈，正前方那一格也不是甜甜圈）， 玩家那 16 个像素一格不变，场上也不出现被抓取的甜甜圈（环0）——这一步除了倒计时以外，玩家什么也没做成。 侧邻或背后躺着一颗自由态(环4)的甜甜圈不碍事。
v1 · 2026-09-16T22:41:21 · L1
ACTION5 打空时（玩家四邻既没有甜甜圈、也没有鼹鼠或被鼹鼠搬着的货），玩家那 16 个像素一格不变，
v1：第1关步1871 要「原地等鼹鼠把它背上那颗送到容器」时抽出。等待也是策略的一步，而『A5 打空等于不动』在册子里一直没人说过——不写它，规划器就只能靠左右横跳来消磨时间，那既踩得到容器空位、也可能挡了鼹鼠的路。全量语料实测：满足 scope 的转移 141 条（第0关110、第1关1、第5关9、第7关20、第8关1），141 条里玩家那 16 个像素全部一格没变、结果帧一次也没出现过环0；另有 5 条转移里别处的货变了环，逐条看都是鼹鼠自己捡起/放下，所以 scope 明写这条不管别处的货。
v1 源码
"""ACTION5 打空时（玩家四邻既没有甜甜圈、也没有鼹鼠或被鼹鼠搬着的货），玩家那 16 个像素一格不变，
场上也不出现被抓取的甜甜圈（环0）——这一步除了倒计时以外，玩家什么也没做成。"""

SCOPE = ("只看 ACTION5；换关/RESET（ctx.level_prev != level_next）返回 NA；me() 定位不到返回 NA；"
         "场上已有环0（正拖着一颗）返回 NA；玩家四邻格里有甜甜圈（任何环）、鼹鼠、或被搬的货，"
         "返回 NA——那几片分别归 donut_grab_toggle 与 action5_vanishes_adjacent_mole 管。"
         "同一步别处的甜甜圈怎么变（鼹鼠捡起或放下）不归这条管，只主张玩家自己那一块和「没抓起任何东西」。")

def _idle_scope(prev):
    """返回玩家格 (r, c)；不在 scope 里返回 None。"""

        return None

        if abs(n[0] - pc[0]) + abs(n[1] - pc[1]) == 1:
            return None

        return "NA"

        return "NA"

        return "FALSE"

        return None

        return None

from _lib import CELL, me, facing, donuts, moles, stolen, cell_px, mask_timer_row

DV = {"up": (-1, 0), "down": (1, 0), "left": (0, -1), "right": (0, 1)}


def _idle_scope(prev):
    """返回玩家格 (r, c)；不在 scope 里返回 None。"""
    if donuts(prev, ring=0):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    fc = facing(prev)
    if a is None or fc is None:
        return None
    pc = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    rings = {(r // CELL, c // CELL): k for r, c, k in donuts(prev)}
    front = (pc[0] + DV[fc][0], pc[1] + DV[fc][1])
    if front in rings:
        return None                      # 正对着货：不表态
    near = [q for q, k in rings.items() if k == 3] + list(moles(prev)) + list(stolen(prev))
    for n in near:
        if abs(n[0] - pc[0]) + abs(n[1] - pc[1]) == 1:
            return None
    return pc


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION5"):
        return "NA"
    pc = _idle_scope(prev)
    if pc is None:
        return "NA"
    if donuts(nxt, ring=0):
        return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if cell_px(prev, pc[0], pc[1]) == cell_px(nxt, pc[0], pc[1]) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION5"):
        return None
    if stolen(prev):
        return None      # 鼹鼠驮着货：它这一步可能把最后一颗投进容器，整帧重画（第7关步1670）
    pc = _idle_scope(prev)
    if pc is None:
        return None
    return mask_timer_row({(pc[0] * CELL + dr, pc[1] * CELL + dc): prev[pc[0] * CELL + dr][pc[1] * CELL + dc]
                           for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)})   # 行15 的第 4 像素行是计时条，它每步在变，不押

ACTION5 打空时（玩家四邻没有鼹鼠、没有被搬的货、没有环3 的甜甜圈，正前方那一格也不是甜甜圈），
v1：第1关步1871 要「原地等鼹鼠把它背上那颗送到容器」时抽出。等待也是策略的一步，而『A5 打空等于不动』在册子里一直没人说过——不写它，规划器就只能靠左右横跳来消磨时间，那既踩得到容器空位、也可能挡了鼹鼠的路。全量语料实测：满足 scope 的转移 141 条（第0关110、第1关1、第5关9、第7关20、第8关1），141 条里玩家那 16 个像素全部一格没变、结果帧一次也没出现过环0；另有 5 条转移里别处的货变了环，逐条看都是鼹鼠自己捡起/放下，所以 scope 明写这条不管别处的货。v2：场上有货在鼹鼠背上时 apply 闭嘴。v1 入册即报 P=0.990（认领 1632 格说错 16），那 16 格是第7关步1670（tid e2185dc1f4148a99）：玩家按 A5 打空，同一步鼹鼠把它驮的最后一颗投进了容器，第7关直接换到第8关，整帧重画。assert_ 靠 ctx 的 level_prev != level_next 返回 NA，apply 看不到结果帧，只能从 prev 上认：驮着货的鼹鼠随时可能投进最后一个位，所以 stolen(prev) 非空就不生成。这一条同时是「鼹鼠送货也能换关」的直接证据。v4（总账记 v4：v2 之后有过一版只改词汇的重结算；第7关步2919 蹲巢时抽出）：巢边总躺着环4，之前的 scope 一碰到它就闭嘴，策略只剩「按被挡的方向键」当空拍，那会把刚对准贼的头转开——蹲点要的恰恰是不转头的空拍。全语料复核（探针 probe_a5_beside_ring4）：手上没货、四邻无鼹鼠/环3/环5、四邻有环4、正前方不是甜甜圈时按 A5，共 9 条（第0关6、第1关1、第7关2 …），9/9 玩家格与朝向一格不变、无环0 出现；有 3 条别处的环4 变了，逐条看都是鼹鼠在远处捡货，本条不管。所以这一版把四邻的排除从「任何甜甜圈」缩到「环3」，另加一条「正前方那一格不是甜甜圈」——正对着货按 A5 语料里一条都没有，不表态。

from _lib import CELL, me, facing, donuts, moles, stolen, cell_px, mask_timer_row

DV = {"up": (-1, 0), "down": (1, 0), "left": (0, -1), "right": (0, 1)}


def _idle_scope(prev):
    """返回玩家格 (r, c)；不在 scope 里返回 None。"""
    if donuts(prev, ring=0):
        return None
    a = me(prev)
    fc = facing(prev)
    if a is None or fc is None:
        return None
    pc = (a[0] // CELL, a[1] // CELL)
    rings = {(r // CELL, c // CELL): k for r, c, k in donuts(prev)}
    front = (pc[0] + DV[fc][0], pc[1] + DV[fc][1])
    if front in rings:
        return None                      # 正对着货：不表态
    near = [q for q, k in rings.items() if k == 3] + list(moles(prev)) + list(stolen(prev))
    for n in near:
        if abs(n[0] - pc[0]) + abs(n[1] - pc[1]) == 1:
            return None
    return pc


def assert_(prev, action, nxt, ctx=None):
    if ctx and ctx["level_prev"] != ctx["level_next"]:
        return "NA"
    if not action.startswith("ACTION5"):
        return "NA"
    pc = _idle_scope(prev)
    if pc is None:
        return "NA"
    if donuts(nxt, ring=0):
        return "FALSE"
    return "TRUE" if cell_px(prev, pc[0], pc[1]) == cell_px(nxt, pc[0], pc[1]) else "FALSE"


def apply(prev, action, ctx=None):
    if not action.startswith("ACTION5"):
        return None
    if stolen(prev):
        return None      # 鼹鼠驮着货：它这一步可能把最后一颗投进容器，整帧重画（第7关步1670）
    pc = _idle_scope(prev)
    if pc is None:
        return None
    return mask_timer_row({(pc[0] * CELL + dr, pc[1] * CELL + dc): prev[pc[0] * CELL + dr][pc[1] * CELL + dc]
                           for dr in range(CELL) for dc in range(CELL)})   # 行15 的第 4 像素行是计时条，它每步在变，不押
